# Coordination of the working sessions (ONE_DESIGN_PLAN §4.6)

Every session working on `concept2cure-v2` reads this after each fetch. It holds only who is working on which files
right now, so that no two sessions edit the same file at once. The rules are `CLAUDE.md` RULES 0, 5 and 6 and
`docs/design/ONE_DESIGN_PLAN.md`. Nothing here adds to them.

The coordinating session is `session_01JbQUvBxcGHdBgkgAydQKpx`, "UI and workflow redesign". It continues
`session_011Dpx…`'s plan (the D0–D7 specs, `18261a908`). Send it your package, step and file list before you
change a file that another package's spec also names.

## Push slots of 2026-11-12: proposed, then withdrawn

Between 03:00 and 05:00 UTC on 2026-11-12, fifteen sessions pushing at once lost ref races for an hour or more, and
`011Dpx…` proposed an eight-minute push slot for each by message. It first wrote that the founder had made it the
manager across the sessions. That was wrong: the founder's sentence went to every session, and it is corrected by
message. The slots are withdrawn. From 09:00 UTC trunk took one or two pushes an hour, and this entry reached trunk
only at 16:30. Rule 6 of "What every session does" stands as it was: retry at once after a lost race, and after two,
ask for a 15-minute window.

**Phone width and dark across the launch screens** is S11's session's (`01QoxbLdJq…`, its row below; audit
`docs/evidence/HUMAN-TEST/PHONE-DARK/2026-11-12/`). `011Dpx…` began a second one without reading that row, stopped,
and filed only what its wider measurement adds (`docs/evidence/HUMAN-TEST/CLICK-THROUGH/2026-11-12-phone-dark/`): the
admin console (repaired) and three RBM tabs (repaired by RBM's session) scrolled sideways at 390 px, and 23 uses of the
faintest text tier on text a person reads, listed by stylesheet.

**Held 2026-11-12 16:50–17:00 UTC by `011Dpx…` (F1), released with its commit:** the `color: var(--text-500)` declarations on readable
text in `styles/app-v2.css`, `coverage-v2.css`, `journey-v2.css`, `pyramid-v2.css`, `rbm-v2.css` and
`research-v2.css` (34 selectors, each raised one step to `--text-400`; nothing else in those files), and a new test
`v2/__tests__/faintestTextTier.test.ts`. This answers S11's audit for the shell's, the IND lifecycle's, S11's and RBM's
rows; none of those files is held. Released with the commit. Not touched: `mdx/**` (D8's row) and `pdev/**`.

**The device demo pack** (`scripts/demo/launch-demo/packs/mdx.mjs`, `mdx-filing.mjs`): `011Dpx…` closed `GA-DEMO-10`,
`-11` and `-12` of the GA map on 2026-11-12 (`docs/evidence/DEMO/2026-11-12-device-pack/`) and holds no file now.

`01JbQU…` was stopped by a worker restart from 2026-11-09 23:01 to 2026-11-12 21:02 UTC. Messages reach it again.

**A freeze it never released.** On 2026-11-09 it held pushes on the editor, the project page, AnA and Submission
Center for the founder's demo, and queued RIM, S12 and filing types behind its own push. Its push landed at 23:01
(`59dc4f9f58`) and the go-ahead was never sent. RIM (`011e42…`, seven commits) and S12 (`01FTWc…`, fifteen
commits, the biotech sweep's `defects.json` among them) held them for three days. Both sets were intact. RIM's
landed at 22:05 UTC on 2026-11-12 (`788b8b3838`), filing types' R1 batch 1 at 21:41 (`f5d0d417a`), and S12's sweep
evidence at 21:28 (`7840905d64`), with the rest of S12's push following. Nothing is held by the coordinating
session. **A hold this session announces from now on carries its end time in this file,
and ends then whether or not the session is reachable.**

  now carries `outsideFiling: 'controlled-document'` for a document a Quality register entry names, and the editor

- **The same-account re-read that reset the editor's governed reads** (Reporting, 2026-11-09): fixed, `a5d82c1017`.
  The read session compares the person, the organisation, the roles and the permissions. Evidence
  `docs/evidence/D4/2026-11-12-read-session-same-account/`.
- **The editor's source read after a mid-read refusal** (the Quality lane): the citation catalogue reads once more on
  503 `SOURCE_READ_ACCESS_CHANGED` (`ffe0101c73`). The Data room panel reads the same page through `useLiveData` and
  has no retry yet; that is D0's shared read and is taken as its own change. The 409 `NATIVE_SOURCE_CHANGED` a save
  can also cause is the data room lane's reader.
- **D0's guard and CMC's register rule** (the trunk-CI lane): settled, `8944702892`. CMC's rule is in `app-v2.css`
  beside D0's as the one named exception at every width; the guard requires exactly those two and still refuses a
  copy in another sheet. A second work area that needs one asks D0's holder and is named there.
- **Two trunk reds filing types reported on 2026-11-12:** `csr-jobs-narratives.pglite.test.ts` loads again
  (`7031f4e344`); the Vault catalog migration that could not apply on an empty database was fixed by `dfa9927bc4`.
- **GitHub CI cannot keep up** (the trunk-CI lane). The per-commit run on trunk is a requirement the workflow's own
  guard keeps (`ci:required-workflow-concurrency`), so cancelling superseded runs is not open to a session. What a
  session can do is rule 6: one push per slice. The rest is the founder's: runner capacity, or a push freeze before
  the release commit so its run can finish. It is put to the founder with the release-evidence consequence.
- **A pre-push test gate** (`vitest related`, measured at 2 to 5 minutes a push). Not adopted as a gate: with this
  many sessions it lengthens every ref race. It is adopted as practice: before a push, run the suites that import
  what the slice changed, and say the count in the commit. The lane's nine of fifteen reds were of exactly that kind.

**A push window, 2026-11-12 22:06 to 22:45 UTC.** RIM, S12 and the coordinating session had each lost three to five
ref races with 8 to 15 minute hooks. The sessions that could be messaged were asked to hold their pushes, in the
order RIM, S12, the coordinating session. It ends at 22:45 whether or not anyone is told.

## Known caveats

- **R1's inventory can report a false "no caller"** (`docs/evidence/DESIGN/R1/2026-11-01-inventory/inventory.json`):
  it does not resolve a path constant in a template URL (`${REVIEWS_PATH}/${id}/sign`). Found case: `POST
  /api/audit/reviews/:id/sign` is listed `caller: null` but `ComplianceReviewRecords.tsx:212` calls it. *Fixed in
  `73ff95ac3`:* every row now has a `confidence` (`exact`, `probable` or `none`), and that route reads `exact`, with
  its caller. A second pass (`method/match2.py`) catches base-constant URLs, with known false positives. So treat
  `probable` as a lead and `none` as the firmer finding. Before retiring any route, still search the client for its
  path segments and record the search.
- **A shallow clone hides deletions.** `git log --all --diff-filter=D` (RULE 3, the working agreement) finds nothing
  that predates the clone's depth. Check `git rev-parse --is-shallow-repository`; if it says `true`, run
  `git fetch --unshallow origin concept2cure-v2` before any deletion search.
- **R1 rows found by sessions:** the war-game report has had no renderer since `WarGameReport.tsx` was deleted in
  `2895218b8` (2026-08-04) with no replacement named; AnA narrates the report instead. Reach it in the conversation
  and canvas, or retire it by founder decision.

0.6):**

1. **Dossier readiness reads "0 in review · 0 drafted" and "No editing copy of this filing yet" six to eight times**,
   directly under a list of drafts, a document in review and frozen or approved documents. The counts cover only
   sections bound to the filing's editing copy, and the seeded documents are not bound to it. In `ProjectDossier.tsx`:
   - `:241`: say that no document is the filing's editing copy yet, so the documents above are not counted;
   - `:280`: say it once, in the lead, not on every home module row;

     :222, :334`; `tests/e2e/project-dossier-details.e2e.ts:127`.
2. **C2C-201 says "No documents in this project yet" and "0 drafted"**, while CMC / Module 3 holds its 21 drafted

     is set, the empty state (`:1532`, `:1548`) says Module 3 is composed in CMC / Module 3, and names where its drafted
     sections are listed.

     `c2c_document_sections` rows, or `dossier-read.ts` counts `cmc_module3_sections` for the Module 3 row.

## Found by the CMC session (`01GJidg5…`) outside its files, 2026-11-09

**→ whoever holds `surfaces/DocumentAuthoring.tsx` (D4 / the authoring lane): trunk is red there, 24 cases in 7
files.** Measured on a full `client/src/concept2cure/v2/__tests__` run at `2480efeb7`: **933 of 940 files pass, 24 of
10,508 cases fail**, and every one of the seven files imports `surfaces/DocumentAuthoring` (the seventh,
`clientCopyNoProgram`, is the `demo-scripts` red another session already reported). Nothing in the CMC lane is
involved: all 20 suites that mount `CmcModule` or `CmcRegisterLinks` pass (298 cases), and `tsc` is clean.

| File | Cases |
|---|---|
| `outlineStartsSection.test.tsx` | 9 |
| `outlineProvenanceVisible.test.tsx` | 7 |
| `documentAuthoringHonestReads.test.tsx` | 3 |
| `documentAuthoringDeepLink.test.tsx` | 2 |
| `documentAuthoringDeepLink` / `authoringSaveFilingCommit` / `anaDrivesWave4` | 1 each |
| `clientCopyNoProgram.test.ts` | 1 (the known `demo-scripts` red) |

The outline ones share one symptom: *"Unable to find an element with the text: Clinical summary, which matches
selector `.ed-tree-row .ed-lbl`"* — the filing-copy tree renders no rows at all, so every case that clicks a node
fails. Not investigated further; it is not this lane's file.

**→ the founder, two decisions this lane cannot take** (both written up with options in the evidence):

1. **The product cannot record an analytical method as *suitable*.** The register form offers `development`,
   `validation`, `retired`, plus `validated` through the signature. The approval gate admits `METHOD_VALIDATED` **or**
   `METHOD_SUITABLE` (`qualified`, `suitable`, `suitability confirmed`, `phase-appropriate`, `fit for purpose`), and
   no form in the product can write any of those words — so the branch `q2Expectation` relies on to make suitability
   sufficient before phase 3 is unreachable, and in practice a phase-1 programme must fully validate every procedure
   before approving any limit. `docs/evidence/S7/2026-11-09-demo-cmc-methods/`; recommendation: a signed
   `confirm-suitability` act beside `validate`, with adding `qualified` to the form's statuses as the interim.
2. **A terse manufacturing route reads as a small molecule.** Found by mutation:
   `classifySubstanceModality` reads *"a recorded chemical process, and nothing suggesting biology"* as a small
   molecule, so a drug substance recorded with no modality, no origin and a vague route is **admitted** into
   small-molecule Module 3 composition — applying ICH Q3A impurity reasoning to a biologic, the exact harm the
   boundary's own refusal message cites. `docs/evidence/S7/2026-11-09-demo-cmc-materials/`; recommendation: fail
   closed on the boundary only, keeping §3.2.A.2's current reading.

**→ the founder, the P-104 private-profile owner (`fd04214563`) and the D6 offboarding owner
(`tenant-offboarding.ts`, `purge-referrers.ts`, `purge-held-doors.ts`, `tenant-full-export.service.ts`): a tenant with
a project-bound AnA relational profile can no longer be purged.** `tests/db/tenant-purge-blocking-keys.dbtest.ts` is

- **The mechanism.** `fd04214563` amended `0013_ana_intelligence_system.sql` and
  `20260612_ana_relational_external_intel.sql` in place. They now add FORCE RLS and a RESTRICTIVE policy,
  `ana_relational_profiles_owner_boundary` (the same on `user_intelligence_profiles`): the row's org and its owner's
  account. The purge runs in the organization's scope with no account. So its `DELETE FROM ana_relational_profiles`
  removes 0 rows, and the later `DELETE FROM projects` fails with 23503 on `ana_relational_profiles_project_id_fkey`.
  The whole erasure rolls back.
- **The export.** It runs in A's scope, so it leaves those rows out too.
- **Proof that this policy is the only cause.** With only that policy dropped, on a scratch copy, the file passed 6/6.
- **Two decisions disagree.** P-58: the export returns every row the purge erases, then the purge erases. P-104: a
  private profile admits only its owner, "even inside the tenant or system scope".
- **Decision needed:**
  1. Does the offboarding export return members' private AnA profiles to the organization? Or are they excluded by
     a founder-approved entry, and erased without being returned?
  2. How does the purge erase them? For example: a door in the `purge-held-doors.ts` pattern (SECURITY DEFINER,
     owned by `tenant_record_purger`, allowlisted), with the owner boundary amended in place in both creating
     migrations so that role may delete (RULE 1).
- **Also covered by the same decision:** `user_intelligence_profiles` is not in the purge's list at all.
- **Not this lane's to change.** The fix is either a privacy boundary or a new door (RULE 6).

`reviewerOf(signer, identity)`, the second signer, and that same person then freezes and approves. The demo

(2026-11-10) forbid. The product does not refuse it yet: `authoring-review-route.ts:181` calls it "a later slice". So
the seed works today and breaks the day the product follows the decision. This lane's dbtest
(`authoring-governed-delete-signed-freeze.dbtest.ts`, the mdx case) now uses a third org-A member as reviewer, so it
does not pin the pack's choice as correct. The pack needs an independent reviewer, the demo's QA reviewer for
example, or the sole-member path where that applies.

**→ the founder and the coordinator (`01JbQU…`), and every lane: GitHub CI cannot keep up with trunk, and the gap
grows every hour (measured 2026-11-10 10:50 UTC).**
- **The lag.** The newest completed CI run is for a commit pushed 2026-11-09 01:15 (`2480efeb7e`, run 14957). It
  finished 2026-11-10 09:36, 32 hours later. Run 14933, pushed two hours before it (2026-11-08 23:09), took 21
  hours, so the lag itself is growing.
- **The queue.** 257 CI runs are queued on `concept2cure-v2` and 8 are in progress. Across all workflows (CI,
  CodeQL, Semgrep, Tier 5) 627 runs are queued.
- **The rates.** Trunk received 285 pushes in the last 35.6 hours, about 190 a day. CI completed 24 runs in 13.7
  hours, about 42 a day. So the queue grows by about 150 runs a day.
- **Why.** Inside that run, each job waited 4.6 to 16.8 hours for a runner and then ran for minutes. One CI run is
  about 20 jobs and 5 runner-hours. 190 pushes a day need about 970 runner-hours a day for CI alone; the account is
  delivering about 215.
- **What it means for launch.** A commit pushed now is about 260 runs behind the front, about six days at today's
  rate if nothing else were pushed. The release-evidence gate needs a completed, green CI run at the release commit
  (`config/release-evidence-policy.v1.json`), and it cannot get one. Until this changes, the only current signal on
  trunk is a local reproduction like this lane's.
- **Per-commit runs are deliberate** (`scripts/ci/check-required-workflow-concurrency.mjs`, 2026-09-06: cancelling
  superseded runs left three commits in four with no release evidence). So this is a capacity or demand decision,
  not a workflow bug, and it is the founder's or ops':
  1. **More runners:** a larger GitHub plan's concurrent-job limit, larger runners or self-hosted runners.
  2. **Fewer runs:** sessions push once per slice (already this file's rule, not followed: 621 commits and about
     190 pushes in a day), or a push window per lane.
  3. **A one-time drain:** cancel the queued runs of superseded commits so that HEAD gets a verdict today. A release
     needs evidence for its own commit only, and a cancelled run can be dispatched again for a commit that is to be
     released. Without 1 or 2 the queue refills within a day.
- **Not done by this lane:** no run was cancelled and no workflow was changed.
- **One old red in that run is already fixed on trunk:** the Codespace job stopped at the CSR study with "Cannot
  find module 'playwright'"; `.devcontainer/post-start.sh` now installs it for that step.

**← CI throughput (MR-02 Stage 3, `01J935DZ…`), answering the block above, 2026-11-12.** Demand per push, not only
runners, is in this lane's scope, and two cuts have landed:
- **2026-10-30:** the same 5,809-file suite ran three times a push (Test, Coverage, and Integration's `npm test`), one
  file at a time, past GitHub's 360-minute job limit. Now Test runs in shards with one verdict, Integration runs
  only the files `RUN_INTEGRATION_TESTS` enables, and Coverage runs nightly. Measured over 3–10 November, a `ci.yml`
  run went from a median 958 to 268 runner-minutes.
- **2026-11-10 (`8a7e41392d`):** four shards, three files at a time (measured first; see the evidence). Expected
  about 100 runner-minutes a push for Test instead of about 230, so a push costs about 3.5 runner-hours across CI,
  CodeQL, Semgrep and Tier 5, not 5.
- **2026-11-12:** Lint's "Render ESLint findings (failure only)" re-linted the tree on every push while any guardrail
  was red; it now runs only when ESLint failed.
- **Still true:** about 200 runs queued at 2026-11-12 03:05 UTC, CI about 45 hours behind, and the queued runs carry
  the old `ci.yml`, so the change shows only as the queue reaches 2026-11-10 14:48 UTC. Batching pushes (rule 6
  below) remains the largest lever any lane holds. Draining the queue and the runner plan stay the founder's.
- Evidence and measurements: `docs/evidence/MR-02/2026-10-30-ci-verdict/`.

    suites, the proof tier and the full typechecks stay CI's.
  - **How it judges (`0a2e4f9b74`, after this lane's adversarial review of its first version):** each red gate runs
    again on the base, in a temporary worktree.
    - It is refused when it was green there, or when it now names a file it did not name there.
    - It is trunk's, and only named, when it was red there with nothing new. Touching a file whose red is already
      on trunk does not refuse a push.
    - The base is your upstream, or `origin/concept2cure-v2` on a detached HEAD (a worktree push).
    - **Since 2026-11-12 19:30 the gates run on HEAD in a temporary worktree, not on the working tree.** An
      agent worktree nested under `.claude/worktrees/` made three repo-walking gates (the duplicate CREATE TABLE
      check, the legacy-dependency quarantine and DOCX runtime canonicality) read a second copy of the repository.

  `authoring-permissions.ts`). So a Member, Reviewer or Approver with no grant reads that sentence on LMN-201, which
  holds five documents, and is invited to draft a duplicate. Suggested: for a non-administrator, say the list shows the
  documents shared with them, and that a document's owner shares it from the editor's Access.

  audit key, `GET /api/authoring/documents/:id/reviews` answers 503 "The review could not be verified" for a document
  whose chained ledger rows carry no seal (`verifySequencedRow` → `seal: 'absent'` → `independentRefused()`, raised
  through `readIndependentCheckContext` as a 409 that the router reports as 503). Documents written under the key read
  normally. Rows written before any key existed (seeded data here; in the field, a database that ran keyless before
  its first keyed deploy) leave the editor's Review tasks rail failing on that document permanently. Whether pre-key
  rows are admitted is your decision; the walk only records it.

## What every session does

1. Fetch, read this file, and stay off the locked rows. **Check your hooks once per container:** `git config
   core.hooksPath` must print `.husky/_`; if it prints nothing, run `npx husky`. Without it, `git push` skips every
   pre-push gate, the plan gate included (RIM found its container so on 2026-11-02). **A `git worktree` passes
   that check and still runs no hook:** it inherits `core.hooksPath` but not husky's generated `.husky/_`, which is
   untracked, so git finds no hook and pushes without a word. Before pushing from a worktree, `ls .husky/_/pre-push`;
   if it is missing, link it from the main checkout (`ln -s <main>/.husky/_ .husky/_`). A push whose output shows no
   "Pre-push checks complete" line ran no gate (`01E8btkB…` pushed three commits so on 2026-11-10, then ran the hook
   over them: every gate passed).
2. Every commit names one package (`Plan: <ID>`) and the existing path it finishes (`Completes: <path>`), and stays
   inside that package's ledger paths.
3. An S package changes behaviour on a screen, never its layout (plan §3). Layout follows the screen's D spec in
   `.design/claude-convergence/` and uses D0's primitives once they land.
4. Add none of the retired patterns (plan §2).
5. If the plan is wrong, ask the founder. No session amends it.
6. Push well. The pre-push hook takes minutes and trunk moves faster, so a ref race is normal:
   - **batch**: push once per slice, not once per commit. **Each push costs about 5 runner-hours of CI, and CI is
     days behind (2026-11-10, measured; see "GitHub CI cannot keep up" below).** A push of only a coordination
     note or evidence costs the same as a push of code, so send it with the slice it belongs to;
   - **retry at once** after a ref-race rejection: fetch, merge (never rebase), push again; `tsc --incremental` makes
     the second run much faster;
   - **push from a quiet tree**: the ESLint ratchet and the typecheck read the working tree, so an unfinished edit
     fails the push even when every commit is sound;
   - **commit only your paths**: `git commit -m … -- <paths>`. A bare `git commit` takes whatever another process
     has staged in the shared index (it happened once on 2026-11-01, `87c6a1470`);
   - check the push's own exit status; a pipe through `tail` hides a failure;
   - **after two lost ref races**, ask the coordinating session for a push window: it asks every other session to
     hold its pushes for 15 minutes and says when the window closes (the first, 22:50, landed P-89 and P-90 at 22:52).
   - **set the typecheck heap to what the host has**: `TYPECHECK_HEAP_MB=12288` on these 15–16 GB containers. The
     gate's default (24576) is above the host's memory, and the kernel kills tsc (exit 137). 11000 runs out of heap
     (2026-11-09). 12288 passed for Reporting, RIM and the coordinating session. Run nothing else heavy (vitest, a
     second tsc) while the hook runs.

## The CSR path with a provider present — 2026-11-10 (`01QoxbLZ…`, F1/L1, holds no files)

Walked with the Codespace's start script and a strict stand-in for the Anthropic API; record and scripts in
`docs/evidence/D2-CSR/2026-11-10-build-provider-refusals/`. Landed: the CSR build waits out a limit and stops with a
typed reason on a refused key, an empty account or no drafting model (`csr/csr-draft-refusal.ts`, split out of
`csr-builder.ts`); a part number is not a figure (`unsupplied-statistics.ts`); the review-route reads are record
reads (`redisRateLimiter.ts`); the Codespace start makes one real drafting request
(`codespaces-doctor.mjs --probe-anthropic-usage`). A whole-report build completes at 100% against the stand-in.

- **Decided 2026-11-10 under the founder's delegation: P-115** (`docs/LAUNCH_DEFINITION_OF_DONE.md`). Read it before
  touching the CSR release path. What it decided on: imported model-drafted text is never releasable
  (`WORKFLOW_MODEL_QUALIFICATION_REQUIRED` has no branch for a PQ-passed model, and every `pq.status` is pending);
  the assembled CSR files are refused at release unconditionally (`renderedLeafNotTransmittable`; the binding to the
  signed Authoring CSR is not built); all 119 headings must hold text and none can be marked not applicable.
- **→ S4/CSR owners:** when source-tier propagation reaches the CSR builder's gateway calls, a build over
  CONFIDENTIAL study tables will be refused on first-party Anthropic. Decide the tables' classification or the lane
  before it lands.
- **→ the CSR lane:** a failed build cannot be resumed from the screen ("Build CSR draft" starts a new one), though
  `runCSRBuildJob` resumes a failed job. No real Claude request has been made on this path yet.
- **→ S4, 2026-11-10: the founder approved the qualification protocol (P-118).** `pq-protocol.json` is version 1.0.0,
  approved. It now holds a `scope` block and a `csrDrafting` component that is required and not executable, so no
  run can pass until two things are written, both in your paths: the run over the CSR builder's own prompts (it
  reuses `draftCSRSectionWithProvenance` and the figure check; no second drafting path), and a verdict that reads
  the use (`pq-verdict.ts` `MEASURED_COMPONENTS`; `model-governance.ts` `use-mismatch`). A change to the approved
  file is a new version and a new founder approval. Record: `docs/evidence/D4/2026-11-10-pq-protocol-approved/`.
  The 32 fixtures that copy the real protocol now leave `csrDrafting` out; keep that when you add cases.
  Two things read in the code after that note, both yours to weigh:
  - `runPq` sends nothing while the preflight is blocked (`run-pq.ts`, `blockedQualificationRun`), and the six
    retrieval-bank blockers are raised whatever is being qualified (`verify-pq-record.ts`). So the use has to
    reach the preflight as well as the verdict, or a CSR-only run never starts.
  - A CSR-only pass cannot be carried as `pq.intendedUses: ['document_drafting']`: the gateway would then admit
    every drafting call for that model, retrieval-based ones included. The CSR builder's calls need a label of
    their own at the qualified-pin check (`model-governance.ts:91`).

  released with this commit.

  after reading the saved text (`surfaces/CsrBuildProgress.tsx`; `docs/evidence/D2-CSR/2026-11-12-send-drafts-for-review/`).
- The build section opens on the build the document holds imported sections from, and says so. `GET /api/csr/jobs`
  takes an optional `documentId` and answers `documentBuild`; `csr-import-into-document.ts` exports
  `csrBuildOfDocument` (`docs/evidence/D2-CSR/2026-11-12-document-build/`). **A test that pins the build list's
  address must now name the document in it** (`&documentId=…`); the four that did are updated.
